# SEO Sweep — 2026-10-13

Audit type: weekly link & crawl sweep (CADENCE.md §3.3). Evaluated against a
fresh production build of `dist/` on `main` @ `8e33aea` — 87 pages: 58 posts,
26 tag pages, the homepage, and 2 about pages. `npm run build` passed before
evaluation. 1,999 (rule, URL) checks across all 41 SEO-PLAYBOOK §§1–6 rules;
**0 unevaluable**.

Weekly-sweep focus — internal-link crawl, outbound-link inventory, JSON-LD
validity, and canonical tags — is **all green**: IL-03 (no broken internal
links), IL-04 (no orphans), MT-03 (self-referential canonical), SD-11 (JSON-LD
parses, no placeholders), and every SD-01/03/05/06/07/08/09/10 structured-data
rule PASS on every in-scope URL. SM-01–SM-06 (sitemap + llms.txt hygiene) PASS.

## Outcome

**Report only — no new fix PR this cycle.** Every mechanical violation found is
already pending in an open draft PR, so re-fixing it would duplicate work the
system already has in flight (CADENCE.md §0.5 idempotency; AGENT.md Invariant 1
— never merge, never deploy):

- All **8 MT-02** over-length meta descriptions are already trimmed in open
  draft PRs **#86** (7 posts) and **#87** (1 post: `the-map-you-didnt-draw`).
  The fixes live on those branches; the long descriptions remain on `main`
  until a human merges them. Not re-fixed here.
- All **7 IL-02** findings are editorial (authoring-time prose work, never an
  auditor prose edit per the brief) and are tracked in open issue **#85**,
  whose current 7-post set is identical to this run. A continuity comment for
  this cycle was posted to #85.
- **HP-04** (1, minor) is a component-driven heading-order artifact — reported
  only, not blocking.

No `human-approval` findings this run: SD-06 passes (no source-repo `sameAs`),
MT-07 is correctly omitted (ratified 2026-07-04 — Waev has no official handle),
and SM-04 llms.txt canon is intact.
